new Error('HTTP '+xhr.status+' '+path);}var data=JSON.parse(xhr.responseText||'{}');if(data.error){throw new Error(data.message||'Pixiv API error');}return data.body||{};}function text(v){if(v===undefined||v===null){return '';}if(typeof v==='string'||typeof v==='number'){return String(v).trim();}return '';}function parseTarget(v){var s=text(v);var sm=s.match(/series-(?:(\\d+)-)?(\\d+)$/)||s.match(/user\\/(\\d+)\\/series\\/(\\d+)/)||s.match(/sid:(\\d+)/);if(sm){return {type:'series',seriesId:text(sm[sm.length-1])};}var im=s.match(/artworks\\/(\\d+)/)||s.match(/aid:(\\d+)/)||s.match(/^(\\d+)$/)||s.match(/(\\d+)/);return im?{type:'illust',illustId:im[1]}:{type:'none'};}function toChapter(i,index){var title=text(i&&i.title)||('第'+(index+1)+'话');var ts=Number(i&&i.upload_timestamp||0);var pageCount=Number(i&&i.page_count||0);return {id:text(i&&i.id),url:text(i&&i.id),title:title,publishTime:ts>0?ts*1000:0,size:pageCount};}function collectSeries(seriesId){var result=[];var lastOrder=0;var guard=0;while(guard<20){var body=api('/touch/ajax/illust/series_content/'+encodeURIComponent(seriesId)+'?last_order='+lastOrder+'&lang=zh');var list=Array.isArray(body.series_contents)?body.series_contents:[];if(list.length===0){break;}result=result.concat(list);lastOrder+=list.length;if(list.length<30){break;}guard++;}return result;}try{var target=parseTarget('144611958');var chapters=[];if(target.type==='series'){var illusts=collectSeries(target.seriesId);illusts.forEach(function(i,index){if(i&&i.id){chapters.push(toChapter(i,index));}});}else if(target.type==='illust'){var body=api('/touch/ajax/illust/details?illust_id='+encodeURIComponent(target.illustId)+'&lang=zh');var illust=body.illust_details||{};if(illust&&illust.id){chapters.push(toChapter(illust,0));}}console.log('[Pixiv API] chapters='+chapters.length);return JSON.stringify(chapters);}catch(e){console.error('[Pixiv API] chapters failed',e);return JSON.stringify([]);}})();"}
